Additionally, we find that mixed-timestep training consistently improves rollout stability relative to timestep-specialist models. Under the hood, GEM-3 is a lightweight neighborhood-attention transformer with ~134M parameters on an equirectangular grid with a number of architectural advancements beyond its predecessor GEM-2. The result is a practical forecasting system that couples near-SOTA medium-range probabilistic skill, stable extended-range rollouts, efficient training and inference, and decision-relevant diagnostics.

</details>


### [176] [A Six-Dimensional Taxonomy of Post-Training Adaptation Techniques with Applications in AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06246)
*Fardin Afdideh,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文献综述系统梳理了后训练适应的文献，提出一个六维分类体系（机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围和模型类型），用于统一比较不同后训练技术，并区分了微调、检索增强和提示等易混淆术语，为技术文档、模型变更追踪和治理分析提供统一的词汇表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练适应已成为现代机器学习实践的核心，涵盖重训练、微调、参数高效适应、对齐、检索增强、模型编辑、反学习、校准和多模态指令微调等多种技术。然而现有文献在不同技术族、模型类别和部署场景之间呈现碎片化状态，难以进行方法间的比较或描述训练后模型的具体修改方式。

Method: 该综述综合了后训练适应文献，提出了一个六维分类体系，按机制、目标、数据需求、持久性、结构范围和模型类型对技术进行分类；区分了微调、检索增强和提示等常被混淆的术语；梳理了从传统机器学习到深度学习、基础模型、大语言模型和多模态大语言模型的适应策略演进路径；并映射了技术间的关系（继承、取代、混合和分层部署栈）。

Result: 该综述建立了统一的后训练适应词汇体系，展示了适应策略的演进脉络，揭示了技术间的继承、取代、混合及分层部署等关系，并可用于支持技术文档撰写、模型变更追踪和治理分析。

Conclusion: 综述指出了后训练适应领域面临的开放挑战，包括评估、可复现性、持久性推理时适应、反学习、多模态适应以及面向治理的后训练工作流等方面。

Abstract: Post-training adaptation has become central to modern machine learning practice and includes techniques such as retraining, fine-tuning, parameter-efficient adaptation, alignment, retrieval augmentation, model editing, unlearning, calibration, and Multimodal Instruction Tuning. However, the literature remains fragmented across technique families, model classes, and deployment contexts, making it difficult to compare methods or describe how a trained model has been modified. This survey synthesizes the post-training adaptation literature and introduces a six-dimensional taxonomy organized by mechanism, goal, data requirement, persistence, structural scope, and model type. The taxonomy distinguishes commonly conflated terms such as fine-tuning, retrieval augmentation, and prompting, and shows how adaptation strategies evolve from traditional machine learning through deep learning, foundation models, large language models, and multimodal large language models. It also maps relationships among techniques, including inheritance, supersession, hybridization, and layered deployment stacks. The resulting vocabulary can support technical documentation, model-change tracking, and governance analysis. The survey concludes by identifying open challenges in evaluation, reproducibility, persistent inference-time adaptation, unlearning, multimodal adaptation, and governance-aware post-training workflows.

</details>


### [177] [MetaboLLM: a metabolomics-specialized large language model for biochemical knowledge integration and predictive metabolite graph construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06253)
*Dohyun Ku,Min Gu Kwak,Francisco J. Pasquel,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaboLLM，一种通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索适配的代谢组学专用大语言模型，以及MetaboLLM-GIN，它将生成的生化描述转换为代谢物图用于患者级预测。MetaboLLM在四个骨干模型家族上优于基础和医学适配模型，MetaboLLM-GIN在两个临床应用中取得最高AUC（应激性高血糖预测0.8616，绝经后激素方案分类0.8123），并且模型解释产生了生物学上有意义的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢组学知识分散在异构资源中，难以转化为可预测的表示。现有方法缺乏将分散的生化知识整合为可计算、可预测形式的有效途径，需要一种能够组织和翻译代谢组学领域知识的模型。

Method: 方法包括两大部分：(1) MetaboLLM：通过持续预训练、监督微调和结构化检索对LLM进行代谢组学领域适配；(2) MetaboLLM-GIN：将LLM生成的生化描述转化为代谢物图，使用图同构网络（GIN）进行患者级预测。研究在四个不同的骨干模型家族上验证。

Result: MetaboLLM在代谢组学知识、关系和描述任务上均优于基础模型和医学适配模型，并能迁移到外部公开基准。MetaboLLM-GIN在冠状动脉旁路移植术后应激性高血糖预测中取得最高AUC（0.8616），在绝经后激素方案分类中取得最高AUC（0.8123），优于传统模型、替代图构建方法以及未适配或非检索LLM配置生成的图。

Conclusion: 领域专用语言模型能够将异构的生化知识组织为可预测且可解释的代谢物图表示，为代谢组学从知识分散到患者级预测应用提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Metabolomics knowledge is distributed across heterogeneous resources and remains difficult to translate into predictive representations. We developed MetaboLLM, a metabolomics-specialized large language model adapted through continual pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and structured retrieval, together with MetaboLLM-GIN, which converts generated biochemical descriptions into metabolite graphs for patient-level prediction using a graph isomorphism network. Across four backbone families, MetaboLLM outperformed corresponding base and medically adapted models on metabolomics knowledge, relational, and description tasks, and transferred to an external public benchmark. MetaboLLM-GIN achieved the highest AUC for stress hyperglycemia prediction after coronary artery bypass grafting (0.8616) and postmenopausal hormone-regimen classification (0.8123), outperforming conventional models, alternative graph constructions, and graphs generated from unadapted or non-retrieval LLM configurations. Model interpretation further produced biologically meaningful findings in both applications. These results show that domain-specialized language models can organize heterogeneous biochemical knowledge into predictive and interpretable metabolite graph representations.

</details>


### [178] [RxnCLF: Contrastive Transformation-Aware Reaction Foundation Model for Improved Reactivity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06259)
*Yiting Zheng,Cheng Fang,Anthony Donofrio,Haote Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RxnCLF，一种基于凝聚反应图（CRG）的自监督对比学习框架，用于反应表示学习。该框架将反应物和产物信息统一到单一图结构中，在170万条Pistachio反应上预训练，学习紧凑且连续的潜空间，捕捉反应中心特征和侧链上下文。在多个产率预测基准（Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化BH偶联、HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成）上微调后，RxnCLF一致优于基于图和序列的基线方法，在R²指标上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应产率预测面临标注数据稀缺和反应空间组合爆炸的挑战，现有字符串、指纹和图基反应编码仅部分捕捉化学转化信息，难以对复杂底物的反应进行准确预测，需要更有效的反应表示学习方法。

Method: RxnCLF采用对比学习框架，基于凝聚反应图（CRG）将反应物与产物信息融合为单一图，以学习显式的转化结构而非离散图。模型在170万条Pistachio反应上自监督预训练，学习变换感知且化学可解释的紧凑连续潜空间，同时捕捉反应中心特征和侧链上下文。

Result: 在多个产率预测基准（包括Buchwald-Hartwig、Pd催化BH偶联、HTE C-N偶联和酰胺形成数据集）上，RxnCLF一致优于图和序列基基线方法，R²指标取得最优性能。

Conclusion: 基于CRG的RxnCLF展现出作为可扩展反应基础模型的潜力，有望推广到更广泛的反应空间，并支持区域选择性预测、对映选择性预测和反应条件优化等下游反应信息学任务。

Abstract: Reaction yield prediction remains challenging because labeled data are scarce and reaction space is both combinatorially large and sparsely populated, limiting the generalization of existing reaction representations. String-, fingerprint-, and graph-based reaction encodings only partially capture chemical transformations, making accurate prediction difficult for reactions with complex substrates. We propose reaction contrastive learning foundation (RxnCLF), a self-supervised contrastive framework for reaction representation learning. RxnCLF is built on a condensed reaction graph (CRG) that unifies reactant and product information into a single graph, enabling the model to learn explicit and enriched transformation structure rather than disconnected graphs. Pretrained on 1.7 million Pistachio reactions, RxnCLF learns a compact and continuous latent space that captures both reaction-center features and broader side chain contexts, making it transformation-aware and chemically interpretable. Fine-tuned on multiple yield prediction benchmarks, including Buchwald-Hartwig, Pd-catalyzed BH coupling, and proprietary HTE C-N coupling and amide formation datasets, RxnCLF consistently outperforms graph and sequence-based baselines, improving R2 and achieving the best performance overall. Our results highlight the promise of CRG-based RxnCLF as a scalable reaction foundation model, with the potential to generalize across broader reaction spaces and support diverse downstream reaction informatics tasks, including regioselectivity prediction, enantioselectivity prediction, and reaction condition optimization.

</details>


### [179] [Hypothesis Testing with Conditional Queries: Learnability and the Value of Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06262)
*Zonghuan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究条件查询模型下自适应测试与非自适应测试的查询复杂度差距。在学习可区分性方面，两个分布类的可区分性成立当且仅当它们的成对条件概率存在正分离；当分离为零时，任何有限查询预算下的最优最坏误差恰好为1/2。在自适应优势方面，任何T查询自适应策略都能被一个使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个预先固定查询的随机非自适应程序模拟，其转录在总变差距离上接近自适应转录。同时构造了匹配下界，证明最坏情况下自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)——即交互可将测试数量减少平方因子，但不会带来指数级优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型评估中，自适应测试可以根据先前响应选择后续测试，而非自适应测试必须预先固定所有测试。核心问题在于：在有限结果空间的条件查询模型中，交互（自适应）究竟能带来多大的查询效率优势？是否如直觉所暗示的存在指数级分支优势？该工作旨在严格刻画自适应与非自适应测试之间的查询复杂度差距。

Method: 该文采用信息论与统计学习理论相结合的框架分析。首先基于成对条件概率的分离性刻画可学习性充要条件。随后为任意T-查询自适应策略构造随机非自适应模拟程序，使用O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个预先选择的配对查询，使模拟转录与自适应转录的总变差距离控制在ρ以内。通过构造匹配下界（常数自适应查询复杂度对Ω_ε(N²)非自适应查询复杂度），确立自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Result: (1) 可学习性与正分离性等价：两分布类可可靠区分当且仅当成对条件概率存在正分离；分离为零时最优最坏误差恰为1/2。(2) 自适应用于非自适应模拟：O(N²(T+log(1/ρ)))个非自适应配对查询即可在总变差距离ρ内模拟任意T查询自适应策略。(3) 匹配下界与自适应差距：构造了自适应查询复杂度为常数但非自适应复杂度为Ω_ε(N²)的分布族，从而最坏固定误差的自适应差距为Θ_ε(N²)。

Conclusion: 交互（自适应测试）能将所需测试数量减少平方因子（Θ(N²)），但交互评估中看似指数级的分支结构并不会转化为指数级的查询优势。这为模型评估设计中自适应与非自适应策略的选择提供了理论指导——当非自适应所需的O(N²)查询可承受时，可以保证与自适应策略几乎相同的效果。

Abstract: Model evaluations may fix all tests before observing any responses or select later tests using earlier responses. We study this choice in a conditional-query model on a finite outcome space $\mathcal{X}$ with $|\mathcal{X}|=N$. We first ask which pairs of distribution classes can be reliably distinguished. We then ask how many additional queries are required to match an adaptive tester when all queried events must be fixed in advance. We show that learnability holds if and only if the two classes have positive separation in their pairwise conditional probabilities. When this separation is zero, the optimal worst-case error is exactly $1/2$ at every finite query budget. For any $T$-query adaptive policy and any $ρ\in (0,1)$, we construct a randomized non-adaptive procedure using $O(N^2(T + \log(1/ρ)))$ pair queries chosen before any response is observed. Its simulated transcript is within $ρ$ in total variation of the adaptive transcript, uniformly over all distributions in the model. We also construct a matching family with constant adaptive query complexity and $Ω_\varepsilon(N^2)$ non-adaptive query complexity. Consequently, the worst-case fixed-error adaptivity gap is $Θ_\varepsilon(N^2)$. Thus interaction can reduce the required number of tests by a quadratic factor, but the apparent exponential branching of an interactive evaluation does not yield an exponential query advantage.

</details>


### [180] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-TULA算法，用于从势函数非光滑、梯度超线性增长且非凸的目标分布中采样。该算法直接在次梯度上操作，利用taming技巧处理超线性区域，并在Wasserstein-2距离下给出了非渐近收敛界，所有常数显式依赖于维度和逆温度。论文还提供了相应的优化问题超额风险估计，并在GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势函数上验证了假设，同时展示了SG-TULA的坐标提升变体在预训练任务上与微调后的AdamW和Muon具有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法在处理同时具有非光滑性、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布时，收敛速率仍不理想，且依赖昂贵的平滑化程序。作者旨在开发一种直接基于次梯度、无需平滑化、能在超线性区域保持稳定的显式离散化方案，并改进现有非渐近收敛速率。

Method: 受taming技术启发，将非光滑势函数的次梯度纳入tamed离散化框架，构造SG-TULA（Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm）。该方案直接作用于次梯度，无需任何平滑化处理；通过taming项抑制超线性梯度引起的离散化不稳定，形成显式稳定格式。随后在Wasserstein-2度量下推导非渐近收敛界，所有常数均显式追踪维度和逆温度的依赖性，并进一步给出优化问题的超额风险界。此外，针对LLM预训练设计了boosted coordinate-wise变体。

Result: 在Wasserstein-2距离下获得了非渐近收敛界的改进，改善了目前基于次梯度的Langevin算法的已知速率，且常数显式依赖于维度和逆温度。在正则化预训练势函数（GPT-2系列LLM）上验证了所有假设并给出了显式常数。实验上，boosted coordinate-wise变体的SG-TULA在预训练中与微调后的AdamW和Muon竞争性相当，而后者目前没有可比的非渐近保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸特性的目标分布提供了一种稳定、高效且理论上有保证的采样方法，在收敛速率上优于现有次梯度Langevin算法，且在实际大规模LLM预训练场景中展现出具有竞争力的实证性能。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [181] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB框架，一个基于注意力的模型，用于估计生存分析中个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）。该方法将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，利用治疗组与对照组之间的成对患者比较，通过不精确概率表示处理右删失观测，并使用可学习的注意力机制聚合成对比较信息。在包含螺旋、钟形和圆形等高维非线性结构的合成数据集上，该模型在各类删失率和治疗效果强度下均优于T-learner、S-learner等元学习基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生存分析主要估计平均治疗效果或群体层面的风险差异，但个体化治疗获益评估对于精准医疗至关重要。现有元学习基线（如T-learner、S-learner）在处理高维非线性特征场景时性能显著退化，且难以直接量化个体层面的治疗获益概率。此外，右删失数据的存在使得精确估计个体治疗效果面临挑战，需要一种既能处理删失又能提供个体化估计的框架。

Method: Surv-IPTB框架的核心方法包括：(1) 将IPTB估计重构为二元分类问题，通过治疗组与对照组患者之间的成对比较（pairwise comparison）来实现；(2) 采用不精确概率（imprecise probability）表示处理右删失观测，将不确定的治理效果表征为区间值概率；(3) 引入带有可学习查询-键（query-key）变换的注意力机制，实现灵活的、数据驱动的成对比较聚合，同时学习删失样本的软类概率。

Result: 在具有复杂非线性结构的合成数据集（包括螺旋、钟形和圆形特征空间）上进行了广泛实验。Surv-IPTB在不同删失率和治疗效应强度下均保持稳健性能，且一致优于配备随机生存森林（RSF）、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的T-learner和S-learner基线，尤其在传统方法表现显著退化的挑战性非线性场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架为生存环境下个性化治疗获益评估提供了可扩展且统计上合理的解决方案。实验结果证实了该方法在复杂非线性场景下的鲁棒性和优越性，代码已公开发布供研究社区使用。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [182] [BaKron: Efficient Quantization with Kronecker-Factored Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06291)
*Johann Birnick,Rayan Saab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BaKron是一个高效的神经网络量化求解器，利用Kronecker分解的Hessian近似信息，通过反对角线并行和递归分治策略，将两测自适应舍入的计算复杂度从O(m²n²)降低到O(mn(m+n))，匹配GPTQ的立方级复杂度同时利用更丰富的曲率信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ风格的自适应舍入通常仅使用输入激活的单侧信息，而Kronecker分解的Hessian近似可以捕获输出坐标间的相关性，但直接在向量化权重域应用GPTQ计算代价过高。需要在保留双侧曲率信息的同时实现高效的求解。

Method: BaKron构建在BoA和YAQA的双侧自适应舍入公式之上，结合反对角线并行与递归分治构造。对于m×n的权重矩阵，采用O(m+n)的顺序步骤，将总工作量从O(m²n²)降至O(mn(m+n))。该方法对基础量化器和Hessian估计器都是模块化的。

Result: BaKron在计算复杂度上匹配GPTQ的立方级缩放，同时利用更丰富的曲率信息。提供了实际基准测试，探索了可用的多种Hessian类型，找到了高效计算这些Hessian的技术，并进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: BaKron通过算法设计上的复杂度优化，使得基于双侧Kronecker-factored Hessian信息的自适应量化为可行方案，在保证模型质量的前提下显著降低了计算开销。

Abstract: We accelerate a family of algorithms for neural network quantization whose geometry is informed by any Kronecker-factored approximation of the Hessian. GPTQ-style adaptive rounding typically uses one-sided information derived from input activations. Two-sided Kronecker-factored Hessian approximations can additionally capture correlations across output coordinates, but applying GPTQ directly in the vectorized weight domain is computationally expensive. Building on the two-sided adaptive-rounding formulation used by BoA and YAQA, we introduce BaKron, an efficient solver that combines anti-diagonal parallelism with a recursive divide-and-conquer construction. For an $m\times n$ weight matrix, BaKron uses $O(m+n)$ sequential steps while reducing the total work from $O(m^2n^2)$ to $O(mn(m+n))$. Thus, it matches the cubic scaling of GPTQ while exploiting richer curvature information. Moreover, BaKron is modular with respect to both the base quantizer and the Hessian estimator. We also provide practical benchmarks, consider a range of Hessians that BaKron can be called with, find an efficient technique to compute these Hessians, and evaluate the algorithm experimentally.

</details>


### [183] [On-Policy Self-Distillation without Any Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06296)
*Yijiang Li,Bingyang Wang,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出U-OPSD，一种完全无监督的在线策略自蒸馏方法，仅利用模型自身生成的多样性样本，通过多数投票构造伪标签并定向纠正模型错误，在多个基准上匹配或超越依赖真实标签的监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略自蒸馏方法（如OPD/OPSD）仍依赖外部监督信号（真实标签、环境反馈或更大模型的指导），不符合真正"自蒸馏"的定义。作者希望探索是否仅通过模型自身生成结果就能实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏。

Method: U-OPSD包含两个核心步骤：(1) 对同一输入采样多条rollout，在自一致性阈值下通过多数投票构造伪解决方案；(2) 将教师分布条件在最短的伪解决方案上，蒸馏到模型最长且错误完成的前缀中，从而在模型"自信地犯错"的位置进行精准自我纠正。

Result: 在Qwen3非思考模式下，AIME24/AIME25/HMMT25/MATH500/AMC23基准上，4B和8B规模分别较基座模型提升8.5%和10.7%，平均超过OPSD 3.2%和2.3%；思考模式下4B超过OPSD 0.9%，8B持平，并分别超越GRPO 0.7%和1.1%。

Conclusion: 仅依赖模型自身生成结果就能实现有效的在线策略自蒸馏，U-OPSD在无需任何外部监督信号的情况下，匹配甚至超越了使用真实标签的监督蒸馏方法，证明了内部一致性作为自蒸馏信号的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy (Self-)Distillation (OPD / OPSD) has shown strong potential for post-training large language models (LLMs). However, existing methods still rely heavily on external supervision, including ground-truth signals, environmental feedback, or guidance from larger models, and therefore fall short of genuine "self"-distillation. In this study, we show that on-policy self-distillation can be achieved using only a model's own generations via internal consistency. We propose Unsupervised On-Policy Self-Distillation (U-OPSD). U-OPSD first samples multiple rollouts and constructs a pseudo-solution by majority vote under a self-consistency threshold. It then conditions a teacher distribution on the shortest pseudo-solution and distills it into prefixes of the model's longest incorrect completion, allowing the model to correct itself precisely where it is confidently wrong. Across diverse benchmarks, base models, and training settings, U-OPSD consistently improves over the base models and matches or surpasses supervised methods with ground truth (GT), such as OPSD and GRPO. On AIME24, AIME25, HMMT25, MATH500, and AMC23, U-OPSD improves over the base model by 8.5% and 10.7% on Qwen3 non-thinking mode at the 4B and 8B scales, respectively, and outperforms OPSD by an average of 3.2% and 2.3%. In thinking mode, U-OPSD remains on par with OPSD, outperforming it by 0.9% at 4B and matching it at 8B, while surpassing GRPO by 0.7% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [184] [RRC: Unlocking Generative Reward Models in LLM Reinforcement Learning via Ranking-Based Reward Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06310)
*Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Yifu Huo,Bei Li,Qiaozhi He,Yan Ding,Xiaoyang Hao,Yuxin Gao,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Tongran Liu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC)方法，解决生成式奖励模型与现有RL算法中标量评分范式不匹配的问题，通过基于相对偏好排名的奖励构建来提升RL训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式奖励模型在响应排序方面表现出色，但在强化学习（RL）中未能充分发挥潜力。分析发现根本原因在于生成式奖励模型的比较性本质与现有RL算法采用的标量评分范式之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出RRC方法，包含两种互补策略：1) 自竞争排名（self-competitive ranking），利用采样响应之间的比较；2) 锚点引导排名（anchor-guided ranking），利用少量参考响应实现可扩展的基于排名的奖励构建。

Result: 在开放式聊天和推理基准上的实验表明，RRC显著改善了使用生成式奖励模型的RL训练，在现有奖励构建方法上取得了一致的提升。

Conclusion: RRC通过从相对偏好排名中推导奖励信号，有效弥合了生成式奖励模型的比较性本质与RL算法标量评分范式之间的鸿沟，释放了生成式奖励模型在RL中的潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in reward modeling show a paradigm shift from discriminative reward models to generative reward models. However, despite their strong capabilities in response ranking, generative reward models have not realized their potential in reinforcement learning (RL). Our analysis reveals that this limitation arises from a mismatch between the comparative nature of generative reward modeling and the scalar scoring paradigm adopted by existing RL algorithms. To bridge this gap, we propose a Ranking-based Reward Construction (RRC) approach, which enables generative reward models to provide more effective RL learning signals by deriving rewards from relative preference rankings. RRC introduces two complementary strategies: self-competitive ranking, which exploits comparisons among sampled responses, and anchor-guided ranking, which enables scalable ranking-based reward construction with a small set of reference responses. Experiments across open-ended chat and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that RRC substantially improves RL training with generative reward models, achieving consistent gains over existing reward construction approaches. Our code can be found at https://github.com/wangclnlp/RRC.

</details>


### [185] [CalibForge: Adversarial Solver Calibration for Scaling Learnable Terminal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06352)
*Fanzhe Meng,Guoxin Chen,Jiale Zhao,Shuang Sun,Zhiyu Lin,Wayne Xin Zhao,Ruihua Song,Ji-Rong Wen,Kai Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalibForge，一种自主终端任务合成系统，利用验证过的求解器行为通过对抗性求解器校准来修订候选任务，构建了5,431个校准的终端任务，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上取得32.58%和47.57%的性能，较基座模型提升最高达24.71个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练终端智能体需要可执行且可验证的任务，但仅保证可行性（可执行验证）无法揭示任务相对于给定求解器设置的学习难度。需要一种方法将任务校准到"可学习区域"，使其对训练真正有效。

Method: 提出CalibForge自主终端任务合成系统，通过两种对抗性求解器校准策略修订候选任务：（1）多求解器校准——针对异构求解器池内的分歧；（2）对比求解器校准——针对指定的强通过/弱失败关系。两种策略都基于演示的可解性来锚定求解器相对的可学习区域。

Result: 构建了5,431个校准的终端任务。消融实验表明两种策略比仅靠作者编写和验证或普通单求解器反馈产生更有效的监督信号。在完整集合上训练的模型在Terminal-Bench 2.0上达到32.58%和47.57%；最大提升分别达到Terminal-Bench 2.0上24.71个百分点、SWE-bench Pro上27.68个百分点、Doc2Repo上30.04个百分点。

Conclusion: 求解器相对的可学习性可作为构造有效且可迁移的智能体训练数据的实用目标，实验结果支持这一方法的有效性。

Abstract: Training terminal agents requires executable and verifiable tasks that are not merely solvable, but appropriately challenging for learning. Executable validation establishes feasibility, yet does not reveal how a task behaves relative to a given solver setting. In this paper, we present CalibForge, an autonomous terminal-task synthesis system that uses verified solver behavior to revise candidate tasks through adversarial solver calibration. Multi-solver calibration targets disagreement within a heterogeneous solver pool, whereas contrastive solver calibration targets a designated strong-pass/weak-fail relation; both operationalize a solver-relative learnable zone anchored in demonstrated solvability. Using CalibForge, we construct 5,431 calibrated terminal tasks. Our ablations show that both strategies yield more effective supervision than authoring and validation alone or ordinary single-solver feedback. Models trained on the full collection achieve 32.58% and 47.57% on Terminal-Bench 2.0. The largest improvements over the corresponding base model reach 24.71 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.0, 27.68 points on SWE-bench Pro, and 30.04 points on Doc2Repo. Together, these results support solver-relative learnability as a practical target for constructing effective and transferable agent training data.

</details>


### [186] [An Optimal Agnostic PAC Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06363)
*Markus Engelund Mathiasen,Jian Qian,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个学习器，在有限VC维假设类上达到了不可知的PAC学习最优风险界，匹配了此前Devroye等人给出的下界，解决了样本复杂度问题（至通用常数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可知PAC学习中有限VC维假设类的统计最优风险界虽然在直觉上已知，但尚未有显式构造的学习器在所有固定L*值上同时匹配已知下界。本文旨在填补这一空白，解决样本复杂度问题。

Method: 针对VC维为d≥1的假设类H，构造了一个统一的学习算法（在摘要中未详细展开其具体机制），该算法对不同L*取值均能达成与下界匹配的风险上界，其上界包含L*·(d+log(1/δ))/n的平方根项以及(d+log(1/δ))/n的线性项。

Result: 学习器以至少1-δ的概率取得满足 L(ĥ) ≤ L* + 7×10^8(√(L*(d+log(1/δ))/n) + (d+log(1/δ))/n) 的风险界，其中常数因子为7×10^8。该界与Devroye、Györfi和Lugosi的下界在任意固定L*处匹配至通用常数。

Conclusion: 本文解决了有限VC维类上不可知PAC学习的样本复杂度问题（在通用常数意义下），证明了在所有固定L*取值下均可实现统计最优的风险上界。

Abstract: Let $H\subseteq\{-1,+1\}^X$ be a class of finite VC dimension $d\ge1$. Writing $L$ for the binary risk and $L^*=\min_{h\in H}L(h)$, we construct a learner achieving the statistically optimal risk bound: from an i.i.d.\ sample of size $n$, for every $0<δ\le 1/2$, with probability at least $1-δ$, \[
  L(\widehat h)
  \le L^*+ 7\cdot10^8\left(
  \sqrt{\frac{L^*(d+\log(1/δ))}{n}}
  +\frac{d+\log(1/δ)}{n}
  \right). \] This settles the sample complexity of agnostic PAC learning up to universal constants at every fixed $L^*$, matching the lower bounds of Devroye, Györfi, and Lugosi [A Probabilistic Theory of Pattern Recognition, Springer, 1996].

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Towards a Risk Assessment of Malicious Skill Files in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05223)
*Rui Yang,Michael Fu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Chetan Arora,Joey Chua*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了企业级自主编码代理的"技能接口"安全漏洞。作者通过敌意技能合成方法，利用6个LLM将471个真实shell命令转化为看似良性的技能文件（共2,826个），针对Gemini CLI和Qwen Code两个企业级代理进行大规模测试。结果显示，Gemini CLI在95.5-96.1%的运行中被成功利用，Qwen Code在71.6-74.0%的运行中受害，而显式安全识别率仅为1.99%。作者提出了一个可复现的评估流程，并通过盲测人类金标准验证（Cohen's kappa=0.85）。研究结论敦促企业在采用编码代理前必须评估和缓解技能接口风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主编码代理已被广泛嵌入企业软件工作流并拥有连接系统的委派权限。其核心的"技能接口"（即代理动态加载的指令和脚本文件夹）在赋予代理专业化的同时，也扩大了攻击面，允许恶意shell命令伪装在自然语言技能文件中。现有研究缺乏对这一攻击面的系统性量化评估，企业亟需了解实际风险水平。

Method: 本文提出三项核心方法：(1) 敌意技能合成方法：使用4个家族的6个LLM将471个真实shell命令转化为看似良性的技能文件，产出2,826个技能基准，映射到11个MITRE ATT&CK战术；(2) 可复现评估流水线：结合运行分层、证据锚定、拒绝否决权和确定性声明意图覆盖，以及三裁判LLM-as-a-judge评审机制，并通过盲测人类金标准验证（kappa=0.85）；(3) 大规模实证表征：对Gemini CLI和Qwen Code两个企业级代理进行5,629次完整运行测试。

Result: Gemini CLI在95.5-96.1%的运行中被成功利用（从多数投票到声明意图修正估计，均处于人类金标准范围内），Qwen Code在71.6-74.0%的运行中被利用；利用成功率几乎不受生成模型影响；显式安全识别仅发生在1.99%的运行中。

Conclusion: 技能接口是编码代理的重大安全隐患，攻击者可将恶意shell命令隐藏在看似良性的技能文件中并在绝大多数情况下得手。企业必须在采用编码代理前评估并缓解技能接口风险。论文公开了代码和数据集。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly embedded in enterprise software workflows with delegated authority over connected systems. Central to this architecture is the agent skills interface: folders of instructions and scripts that agents load dynamically to specialize their behavior. This interface also widens the attack surface, letting malicious shell commands hide within natural-language skill files. We make three contributions. First, an adversarial skill-synthesis method using six LLMs across four families to transform 471 real-world shell commands into benign-appearing skills, released as a benchmark of 2,826 skills mapped to 11 MITRE ATT&CK tactics. Second, a reproducible evaluation pipeline coupling run stratification, evidence anchoring, a refusal veto, and a deterministic declared-intent override with a three-judge LLM-as-a-judge panel, validated against a blind human gold standard (Cohen's kappa = 0.85). Third, a large-scale characterization of two enterprise-grade agents across 5,629 completed runs. Gemini CLI is exploited in 95.5-96.1% of runs and Qwen Code in 71.6-74.0% (raw majority vote to declared-intent-corrected estimate, both within the human gold standard), nearly invariant to the generating model. Explicit safety recognition occurs in only 1.99% of runs. Enterprises must assess and mitigate skill-interface risk before adopting coding agents. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/awsm-research/AgentJailbreak

</details>


### [188] [Improving Debugging in Verification-Aware Languages Through Automated Fault Localization: A Case Study in Dafny](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05399)
*Álvaro Silva,Isabel Amaral,João Pascoal Faria,Alexandra Mendes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究验证感知语言（如Dafny）中的自动故障定位，比较了基于状态和基于反例两种范式，发现基于反例的方法显著优于基于状态的方法，且结构化的单轨迹排序和多轨迹聚合能进一步提升定位效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证感知语言（如Dafny）中的验证失败反馈通常只显示具体条件（如违反后置条件），而非故障根本原因。虽然Dafny的反例功能提供执行轨迹，但每次断言失败只暴露单条失败路径，开发者需要手动检查整条轨迹来定位错误，缺乏高效的自动故障定位方法。

Method: 论文比较了两种故障定位范式：(1) 基于状态定位——复制AutoFix的"快照"方法论，通过推断不变量和谓词来识别可疑程序状态；(2) 基于反例定位——包含一组逐步丰富验证器输出的技术：从原始反例提取，到结构化单轨迹排名，再到多轨迹聚合。使用MutDafny从DafnyBench生成多样化变异体数据集，并用EXAM分数衡量定位有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于反例的方法在该设置下显著优于基于状态的定位。单轨迹的结构化排名相比原始反例输出带来了最大的改进，而多轨迹聚合通过增加覆盖率和减少求解器引入的路径偏差，在鲁棒性和调试实用性方面提供了额外增益。

Conclusion: 有效故障定位在验证感知语言中既依赖于使用反例信息，也依赖于如何对反例信息进行结构化和多样化处理。

Abstract: Verification-aware languages, like Dafny, integrate formal specifications directly into source code to enable static correctness checks. However, when verification fails, the feedback provided is often limited to the specific condition of the error, such as a violated postcondition, rather than the root cause of the fault. While Dafny's counterexample features provide concrete execution traces, these typically expose a single failing path per assertion failure, leaving the developer to manually look through the entire trace to locate the error.
  This paper investigates automated fault localization for verification-aware languages by comparing two paradigms: state-based and counterexample-based localization. Our state-based localization strategy replicates the ``snapshot'' methodology of AutoFix by inferring invariants and predicates to identify suspicious program states. The counterexample-based strategy consists of a family of techniques that progressively enrich the use of verifier output: from raw counterexample extraction, to structured single-trace ranking, and to multi-trace aggregation.
  To validate these methods, we present an evaluation framework using MutDafny to generate a diverse mutant dataset from DafnyBench and measure localization effectiveness using the EXAM score. Our results show that counterexample-based approaches substantially outperform state-based localization in this setting. Structured ranking over a single trace yields the largest improvement over raw counterexample output, while multi-trace aggregation provides additional gains in robustness and debugging utility by increasing coverage and reducing path bias introduced by the solver. These findings demonstrate that effective fault localization in verification-aware languages depends both on using counterexample information, and how that information is structured and diversified.

</details>


### [189] [Reasoning from Traces: Divergence-Guided Agentic Repair of WebAssembly Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05521)
*Liyan Huang,Kaicheng Wang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WasmMend是首个自动修复Native-Wasm功能差异的系统，通过差分轨迹分析定位分歧函数，再引导LLM代理生成补丁，在真实C/C++项目上达到70.0%的修复率，显著优于基线方法的50.2%和54.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebAssembly承诺将C/C++代码库无缝复用为可移植、快速、沙箱化的二进制文件，但实际中交叉编译常导致运行时差异（由库实现差异或编译器bug引起），且根因隐藏于源码之下，即使最先进的基于LLM的修复代理也常常无法修复这些差异。

Method: WasmMend采用两阶段方法：首先，通过新颖的差分轨迹分析方法定位Wasm与native执行首次分歧的函数；然后，基于该定位结果引导LLM代理推理根因并生成消除分歧行为的补丁。

Result: 在真实世界的C/C++项目上，WasmMend达到70.0%的修复率，对比基线agentic方法的50.2%和结合修复时LLM插桩方法的54.5%，验证了分歧引导推理对跨平台修复的价值。

Conclusion: WasmMend通过将无方向的探索转化为聚焦的推理任务，有效提升了Native-Wasm功能差异的自动修复能力，证明了差分引导的LLM推理在跨平台修复中的有效性。

Abstract: WebAssembly (Wasm) promises seamless reuse of C/C++ codebases as portable, fast, sandboxed binaries. In practice, however, this promise often falls short: recent studies show that cross-compiling the same C/C++ source to Wasm and native binaries frequently leads to runtime discrepancies, owing to library implementation differences or compiler bugs. Since the root causes lie in the platform-level runtime and are hidden beneath the source code, even state-of-the-art LLM-based repair agents often fail to fix these discrepancies. In this paper, we present WasmMend, the first system to automatically repair Native-Wasm functional discrepancies. WasmMend converts the undirected exploration to a focused reasoning task in two stages: First, a novel differential trace analysis approach localizes the function where Wasm and native executions initially diverge; guided by this localization, LLM agents then reason about the root causes and generate patches that eliminate the divergent behavior. Experiments on real-world C/C++ projects show that WasmMend achieves a fix rate of 70.0%, compared to 50.2% for the agentic baseline and 54.5\% for the approach augmented with repair-time LLM-based instrumentation, demonstrating the value of divergence-guided reasoning for cross-platform repair.

</details>


### [190] [Exploring Dependence, Overreliance, and Addiction Related Behaviors Associated with Large Language Model Use Among Software Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05561)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Italo Santos,Matheus de Moraes Leça,Cleyton Magalhaes,Mairieli Wessel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对119名软件从业者的探索性调查，探讨了LLMs在专业软件开发中的使用模式，发现参与者主要表现为功能性依赖，部分呈现过度依赖特征（优先使用LLM而非文档或同行咨询），而成瘾相关行为相对少见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs已广泛融入软件工程师日常开发工作，但其在专业工作流中可能引发的依赖、过度依赖和成瘾相关行为尚未得到充分研究，需要系统性地理解这一问题。

Method: 采用探索性调查方法，对119名软件从业者进行问卷调查，结合描述性统计和定性主题分析对参与者的开放式回答进行分析。

Result: 参与者主要表现出功能性依赖，LLMs因提供生产力与效率而融入日常软件工程活动；部分模式与过度依赖一致，表现为优先使用LLM而非文档或同行咨询但仍会验证生成输出；成瘾相关行为报告较少，主要表现为难以调节使用或对技术的情感依恋而非控制力受损。

Conclusion: LLMs正成为专业软件工程实践的习惯性组成部分，大多数使用是功能性的，但应通过支持信任校准、专业判断和验证来促进合理依赖。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has changed how software engineers perform everyday development activities. While these systems provide substantial support for tasks such as code generation, debugging, and documentation, their increasing integration into professional workflows has also raised questions regarding developers' reliance on these tools and the emergence of dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. This study investigates how software engineers experience the use of LLMs during professional software development, with attention to behavioral patterns associated with dependence, overreliance, and addiction-related behaviors. An exploratory survey was conducted with 119 software practitioners. The data were analyzed using descriptive statistics and qualitative thematic analysis of participants' open-ended responses. Participants primarily described functional dependence, with LLMs becoming integrated into routine software engineering activities because of the productivity and efficiency they provide. Responses also suggested patterns consistent with overreliance, particularly through prioritizing LLMs over documentation or peer consultation while continuing to verify generated outputs. Reports associated with addiction-related behaviors were less common and primarily reflected difficulty moderating use or emotional attachment to the technology rather than impaired control. The findings suggest that LLMs are becoming a habitual component of professional software engineering practice. While most reported use appears functional, the results indicate the importance of promoting appropriate reliance by supporting trust calibration, professional judgment, and verification throughout software development.

</details>


### [191] [JTA: Joint Testability Architecture for Scenario-Based Validation of Safety-Critical Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05594)
*Wenyao Xue,Jiandi Wang,Yichen Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出联合可测试性架构（JTA），将场景、测试系统与被测系统视为统一的整体进行分析与设计，从可控性、可观测性和可隔离性三个维度刻画验证能力，并引入场景契约、联合能力评估、验证盲区识别和桥接导向的设计行动来系统化地推理安全关键软件的场景化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可测试性研究主要以工件为中心（artifact-centric），缺乏对场景、测试系统与被测系统三者联合能力的架构级支持。安全关键软件验证不仅取决于被测系统本身，还要求关键场景在受控条件下构建、执行证据对齐为可判定的形式、异常结果可归因到可操作的原因，而这些需求现有方法难以系统满足。

Method: JTA将场景、测试系统和被测系统视为单一分析设计对象，沿三个维度（可控性、可观测性、可隔离性）刻画验证能力，通过三个域、三座桥以及分析-设计-评估-优化循环组织这些维度。具体引入场景契约、联合能力评估、验证盲区识别，以及面向桥的设计行动（将能力缺口映射为控制点、证据组织和归因边界的具体改进）。

Result: 对ArduPilot失效保护验证的示例分析表明：链路丢失（link-loss）场景相对成熟，而状态估计异常场景因证据对齐和归因语义较弱而更难验证。JTA不是对现有测试或安全分析技术的替代，而是为安全关键软件中场景化验证的建模、设计与评估提供架构基础。

Conclusion: JTA填补了可测试性研究中缺少架构级联合能力推理的空白，将场景、测试系统与被测系统整合为统一的分析单元，为安全关键软件的验证充分性评估提供了系统化的架构框架。

Abstract: Validation adequacy in safety-critical software depends on more than the system under test. Critical scenarios must be constructed under controlled conditions, execution evidence must be aligned into verdict-ready form, and abnormal outcomes must be attributable to actionable causes. Existing testability research remains largely artifact-centric and offers little architectural support for reasoning about the combined capability of the scenario, the test system, and the system under test. Joint Testability Architecture (JTA) addresses this gap by treating those three elements as a single object of analysis and design. It characterizes validation capability along three dimensions--controllability, observability, and isolability--and organizes them through three domains, three bridges, and an analysis-design-evaluation-refinement loop. JTA also introduces scenario contracts, joint capability assessment, validation blind-spot identification, and bridge-oriented design actions that map capability gaps to concrete improvements in control points, evidence organization, and attribution boundaries. An illustrative analysis of ArduPilot failsafe validation shows that link-loss scenarios are comparatively mature, whereas state-estimation anomaly scenarios remain harder to validate because evidence alignment and attribution semantics are weaker. JTA is not a replacement for existing testing or safety-analysis techniques; it provides an architectural basis for modeling, designing, and assessing scenario-based validation in safety-critical software.

</details>


### [192] [Towards Competence-Based Management for Open Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05599)
*Sabahat Younas,Márcia Moraes,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过收集贡献者提交代码的度量指标来评估其能力水平，并提出了一个能够预测解决任务所需能力等级的模型，以帮助开源软件项目识别和培养可替代核心贡献者的新贡献者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件项目中核心贡献者脱离项目的情况日益严重，威胁到项目生存。寻找新贡献者面临挑战，因为缺乏对潜在候选人能力的精确了解，以往研究虽提供了能力指标的指示，但对能力水平的理解深度不足，导致对贡献者能力的认知不准确。

Method: 通过收集贡献者提交的源代码相关代码度量指标来评估贡献者能力，并提出一个能力模型来预测解决任务所需的能力等级，以准确评估贡献者能力。

Result: 提出了一个能够预测解决任务所需能力等级的能力模型，并提供了包含代码、数据和文档的复现包（DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.21605122）。

Conclusion: 通过准确评估贡献者能力，可以识别并培养候选人来替代核心贡献者，从而维持开源社区和项目的健康发展。

Abstract: Contributors to Open Source Software (OSS) projects are vital to maintaining the health of both communities and projects. However, the number of projects experiencing core contributors' disengagement has increased to the point that it risks the projects' survival. Finding new contributors to replace the workforce is challenging and time-intensive due to several factors, including a lack of precise knowledge about potential candidates' competences, which may need to be confirmed through interviews and exams. Previous studies provided indications of the contributor's competences, although they lack depth in understanding competence levels, which can result in poor knowledge about the contributor's capabilities. To address this gap, we assess contributors' competence by collecting code metrics related to source code from contributions. We also propose a competence model able to predict the competence level required to solve tasks. By properly assessing contributors' competences, we can identify and train candidates to replace core contributors. Our replication package, including code, data, and documentation, is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21605122

</details>


### [193] [Keeping Models and Code in Sync: Roundtrip Engineering for Tactical Domain-Driven Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05612)
*Weixing Zhang,Mario Herb,Wai Chung Dorothy Cheng,Michael Wagner,Bowen Jiang,Tianhai Liu,Anne Koziolek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出JDomInO，一个用于战术领域驱动设计（DDD）的双向同步工具链，通过共享元模型保持Java代码库与领域模型的一致性，支持前向（模型生成代码）和反向（代码重建模型）两个方向的同步，并探讨了结构化领域模型作为AI代码助手上下文层的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDD为核心业务逻辑提供了共享词汇，但实践中领域模型与代码之间常发生漂移——代码变更超过模型更新，或模型修订未落到代码库中，导致二者失去一致性。

Method: JDomInO构建了一个共享元模型，包含12种建筑块（building block）类型。前向路径从领域模型确定性生成Java代码结构，反向路径从现有Java代码重建领域模型。前向路径在酒店管理场景中完成全面验证，反向路径的映射逻辑通过单元测试。

Result: 前向路径已在酒店管理场景中覆盖全部12种建筑块类型并完成验证；反向路径的映射逻辑通过单元测试，端到端验证正在进行中。

Conclusion: JDomInO能够保持代码与领域模型同步演化，其产生的结构化领域模型还可作为AI代码助手的精准上下文层，帮助AI理解聚合边界和DDD语义，这是原始源代码难以直接传达的信息。

Abstract: Domain-Driven Design gives teams a shared vocabulary for complex business logic, but that vocabulary only stays useful as long as the model and the code agree with each other. In practice, they drift apart: code changes outpace the model, or model revisions never make it into the codebase. This paper presents JDomInO, a bidirectional synchronization toolchain for tactical DDD that keeps a Java codebase and its domain model connected through a shared metamodel, with the goal of keeping the two in sync as the system evolves. JDomInO generates Java code structure deterministically from a domain model (forward path) and reconstructs a domain model from existing Java code (reverse path). The forward path has been fully validated on a Hotel Management scenario covering all 12 building block types in the metamodel; the reverse path's mapping logic has passed unit testing, with end-to-end validation underway. We also outline how the structured domain model produced by JDomInO could serve as a precision context layer for AI code assistants, helping them respect aggregate boundaries and DDD semantics that raw source code alone does not convey.

</details>


### [194] [Agent-Based Test Assertion Generation via Diverse Perspective Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05822)
*Dong Wang,Qiaoyu Han,Lin Yang,Jianyi Zhou,Guangtai Liang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AssertMate，一种基于多智能体的测试断言生成框架，通过实际值构造、多视角期望值预测和LLM-as-a-Judge协作机制，显著提升了LLM生成测试断言的准确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的断言生成方法（如ChatAssert）存在准确率不高、过度依赖过采样以及一次性提示导致对模型随机性敏感等问题，亟需更可靠和高质量的断言生成方案。

Method: AssertMate包含三个关键组件：(1) 实际值构造：通过静态分析和类型感知启发式方法识别断言目标；(2) 多视角期望值预测：结合代码生成、检索增强生成（RAG）和思维链（CoT）推理三种智能体进行预测；(3) LLM-as-a-Judge协作机制：选择最合适的断言。

Result: 在Defects4J基准上的评估表明，AssertMate在编译成功率和通过率上显著优于最先进技术，并具有更高的缺陷检测能力。与EvoSuite集成进一步验证了其实用性，取得了更好的突变覆盖率和突变杀死数。消融研究证实三个组件各自具有显著且互补的贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了聚合多样化视角能有效增强基于LLM的断言生成效果，为提升测试质量提供了一种可靠的自动化方案。

Abstract: Test assertions are critical elements of unit tests, serving as checkpoints to validate expected behavior and ensure software correctness. Numerous techniques have been proposed to automate assertion generation, with recent progress notably driven by large language models (LLMs). Despite the promise, existing approaches such as ChatAssert suffer from modest accuracy, heavy reliance on oversampling, and vulnerability to model randomness due to one-shot prompting. To address these limitations, we propose AssertMate, a novel agent-based assertion generation framework that enhances the quality and reliability of LLM-generated assertions through three key components: (1) actual value construction that identifies assertion targets via static analysis and type-aware heuristics; (2) multi-perspective expected value prediction using code generation, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning agents; and (3) an LLM-as-a-Judge collaboration mechanism to select the most appropriate assertion. Evaluation on the Defects4J benchmark demonstrates that AssertMate significantly outperforms state-of-the-art techniques in compilation success and pass rates, along with substantially higher bug detection capabilities. Integration with EvoSuite further validates AssertMate's practicality, yielding superior mutation coverage and kill counts. Ablation studies reveal that each of the three components makes a significant and complementary contribution to the overall performance. This work affirms the great potential of aggregating diverse perspectives to enhance the effectiveness of LLM-based assertion generation.

</details>


### [195] [CodeGrep: An RL-Trained Retrieval Agent for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05886)
*Wuya Chen,Yihao yang,Yang Cao,Yue Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeGrep是一个14B参数的检索智能体，通过GRPO端到端训练，能够多轮并行发起grep、glob和read工具调用，为冻结的下游编码智能体提供候选文件，在SWE-Bench Verified上保持甚至提升解决率的同时，将轮数和tokens开销显著降低（分别减少15%和19%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM编码智能体（如Claude Code和OpenHands）存在共同的低效问题：它们在定位需要修改的文件上消耗了大量token预算，而不是真正执行修补。在SWE-Bench Verified上，一个30B的OpenHands智能体平均每个已解决问题需要23轮和631K tokens，其中大量调用浪费在grep、glob和view_file等仓库探索操作上。

Method: 1) 训练一个14B检索智能体CodeGrep，使用GRPO进行端到端训练，支持多轮并行grep、glob和read工具调用；2) 从67K开源智能体轨迹中通过CATM方法挖掘监督信号；3) 构建Git-worktree环境以支持多轮智能体强化学习；4) 在advantage层而非reward层应用效率信号，以减少KL漂移。

Result: 在全部500个SWE-Bench Verified实例上，CodeGrep将解决率从25.8%提升到27.0%，同时已解决实例的轮数减少15%、tokens减少19%。检索器的下游效用遵循精确率阈值：BM25（精确率0.375）会削弱智能体，Jina（精确率0.445）效果中性，而CodeGrep（精确率0.677）超过该阈值，检索开始降低回滚成本。

Conclusion: 高效的检索智能体可以显著减少编码智能体的探索开销而不牺牲解决率；精确率是决定检索器下游效用的关键指标；在advantage层应用效率信号比在reward层更有效。作者将发布模型、训练流程、RL环境和评估框架。

Abstract: Modern LLM coding agents such as Claude Code and OpenHands share a common inefficiency: they spend much of their token budget finding the file to patch, rather than patching it. On SWE-Bench Verified, a 30B OpenHands agent averages 23 rounds and 631K tokens per resolved issue, with many calls spent on grep, glob, and view_file during repository exploration. We introduce CodeGrep, a 14B retrieval agent trained end-to-end with GRPO to issue multi-turn parallel grep, glob, and read tool calls and return candidate files to a frozen downstream coding agent.
  On all 500 SWE-Bench Verified instances, CodeGrep preserves resolve rate while substantially improving efficiency: 27.0% versus 25.8% for the no-retrieval baseline, with 15% fewer rounds and 19% fewer tokens on resolved instances. Across retrievers, downstream utility follows a precision threshold: BM25 with precision 0.375 degrades the agent, Jina with precision 0.445 is neutral, and CodeGrep with precision 0.677 crosses the threshold at which retrieval begins to reduce rollout cost. To enable this study, we mine supervision from 67K open-source agent trajectories using CATM and build a Git-worktree environment for multi-turn agent RL. In our setting, applying the efficiency signal at the advantage layer rather than the reward layer reduces KL drift and translates cleanly into downstream efficiency. We will release the model, training pipeline, RL environment, and evaluation harnesses.

</details>


### [196] [Mapping the Emerging Curriculum for AI-Assisted Software Engineering via Syllabus Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05898)
*Francis Geng,Anshul Shah,Mia Chen,Paul Denny,Juho Leinonen,Bill Griswold,Gerald Soosai Raj,Leo Porter*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了23门面向高年级本科生、明确涉及生成式AI在软件工程中应用的课程大纲，通过迭代式定性编码，从学习目标、评估方式、教学主题和使用的AI工具四个维度进行了系统特征化，揭示了这一新兴课程领域的共性与差异，为未来课程设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI编码工具正在重塑专业软件开发实践，大学开始设计相关课程帮助学生适应AI辅助开发工作流。通过分析这些课程大纲，可以获取实证证据，揭示这一新兴课程领域如何被定义，并为未来课程设计提供指引。

Method: 收集23份公开可获取的高年级学分制课程大纲及配套材料（筛选标准包括明确涉及生成式AI在软件工程中的应用），采用迭代式定性编码方法，对课程的学习目标、评估方式、教学内容主题以及文档中记录的AI工具进行系统化分类和特征化分析。

Result: 分析揭示了这些课程在多个维度上的共性与差异，为研究人员和教育工作者提供了关于当前课程设计实践的系统性认识，识别出该新兴课程领域的特征模式和多样化路径。

Conclusion: 通过系统化的大纲分析，本研究为高等教育中生成式AI与软件工程交叉课程的建设提供了实证基础，既展示了当前课程设计的现状图谱，也为未来课程开发指明了可借鉴的方向。

Abstract: As Generative AI coding tools reshape professional software development, universities have begun designing courses to prepare students for AI-assisted development workflows. By analyzing the syllabi of these courses, we can gather empirical evidence about these courses, reveal how this emerging curricular area is being defined, and gain guidance for future curriculum design. We analyzed 23 publicly available syllabi and course materials of upper-division, credit-bearing courses that meet specific criteria, including explicitly addressing Generative AI in software engineering. Through iterative qualitative coding, we characterized courses' learning objectives, assessments, topics, and documented AI tools. Our analysis reveals commonalities and differences among these courses that allow researchers and educators to study and develop future courses.

</details>


### [197] [Escaping the Self-Repair Trap: Improving Test Oracle Generation via Dual-Context Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05917)
*Kefan Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DCAware框架，用于回归预言（regression-oracle）生成。现有基于迭代自修复的方法优化执行成功率，但这只是代理目标，与真正发现故障的目标错位，导致"自我修复陷阱"——模型逐渐生成易于满足但检测能力弱的断言。DCAware采用非迭代、计算高效的方案，结合结构化静态上下文与选择性检索的动态状态，无需迭代反馈回路即可生成精准的故障揭示预言，在计算成本显著更低的情况下超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM回归预言生成方法过度依赖迭代自修复和执行反馈，但优化执行成功并不等于产生强故障揭示能力的预言。这种代理目标与实际目标错位，导致自修复过程出现退化现象（"自修复陷阱"），使模型倾向生成容易满足却检测能力弱的断言。

Method: DCAware框架：1) 非迭代设计，避免自修复陷阱；2) 集成结构化静态上下文（static context）与选择性检索的动态状态（dynamic states），提高上下文信噪比；3) 强调高信噪比上下文接地（high signal-to-noise contextual grounding），替代多轮修复。

Result: 基于执行测试和变异测试（mutation testing）的实验表明，DCAware在保持高执行成功率的同时，持续提升故障揭示有效性，且计算成本大幅低于先前的迭代修复方法。

Conclusion: 在研究的回归预言设定下，提升上下文质量比增加迭代修复复杂度更为有效。这暗示预言生成的核心瓶颈在于上下文信息的质量与选择性，而非修复轮数。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for regression-oracle completion, where a test prefix is given and the current program version is treated as expected behavior. Recent approaches increasingly rely on iterative self-repair and execution feedback, but optimizing execution success does not necessarily yield strong fault-revealing oracles. This objective, widely adopted in repair-based methods, serves only as a proxy and may be misaligned with the true goal of oracle generation. Such misalignment biases the repair process, giving rise to a feedback-driven degeneration that we term the Self-Repair Trap, where iterative repair progressively drives models toward assertions that are easier to satisfy but less effective at detecting faults. To address this issue, we propose DCAware, a computationally efficient, non-iterative framework that prioritizes high signal-to-noise contextual grounding over multi-round repair. DCAware integrates structured static context with selectively retrieved dynamic states, enabling precise and robust oracle generation without iterative feedback loops. Extensive experiments based on execution and mutation testing show that DCAware consistently improves fault-revealing effectiveness while maintaining high execution success, outperforming prior methods with substantially lower computational cost. Our results suggest that improving contextual quality is more effective than adding iterative repair complexity in the studied regression-oracle setting.

</details>


### [198] [Expertise-Based Developer Assignment for Long-Term Software Components in Open-Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05919)
*Faheem Ullah,Babar Shah,William Shanks,Ayesha Mohsin,Abrar Ullah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Git提交历史建模开发者专业能力，并自动将开发任务分配给最合适开发者的Web应用系统。系统通过服务器端数据缓存优化性能，测试表明72.4%的任务目标开发者出现在推荐前十名中（共47名候选），相比最差情况速度提升了9.86倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件通过GitHub开展，将正确的任务分配给正确的开发者对团队效率至关重要。分配与开发者专业能力不符的任务会导致完成时间变慢、代码可维护性降低。虽然已有针对特定工作的自动化任务分配研究，但缺乏针对新项目中专业知识相关的长期组件进行开发者分配的研究。

Method: 构建一个Web应用，基于开发者之前的Git提交记录来建模其专业能力，并自动将其分配到新项目中的最优任务。系统采用不同的后端模型，并引入服务器端数据缓存来优化处理速度。

Result: 系统运行速度因后端模型不同而变化，服务器端数据缓存使最差情况下的速度提升了9.86倍。在47名候选开发者中，72.4%的任务将目标开发者列入了推荐前十名。

Conclusion: 该研究表明基于Git提交历史建模开发者专业能力并进行任务自动分配是可行的，且通过数据缓存可有效提升系统性能，推荐质量在top-10范围内具有较高的准确率。

Abstract: Open-source software development through GitHub has enabled countless software projects to be developed by developers from across the world. Assigning the right task to the right developer enables teams to work efficiently. Assigning a task which is not representative of a developer's expertise results in slower completion time and less maintainable code. Whilst much work has been done to automate this assignment for specific jobs, there is little work addressing the assignment of developers to expertise-specific long-term components of a new project. This paper produces a web application that models developers' expertise based on their previous Git commits and automatically assigns them to an optimal task within a new project. Testing showed that the system's speed varies depending on the back-end model used but server-side data caching improved worst-case speeds by a factor of 9.86. 72.4% of tasks had their target developer listed in the top 10, out of a possible 47.

</details>


### [199] [Sensor-Level Fault Diagnosis for Automotive Software Validation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05921)
*Mohammad Abboush,Hamza Ouarrad,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了开源指令微调大语言模型（LLM）在硬件在环（HIL）平台上的汽车软件预系列验证中，作为数据高效且可解释的故障检测与诊断引擎的可行性。提出两阶段框架：先在dSPACE实时平台上自动进行需求检查以筛选违规记录，再通过4比特低秩适配的LLM将信号窗口的统计、关系与上下文描述符映射到故障位置并附带书面解释。在汽油机案例（六种注入故障）中，最小模型（2B参数）达到与最大模型（8B参数）相同的81.6%准确率，且整个适配与评估周期可在单个商用加速器上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HIL平台上汽车软件验证产生大量多元传感器记录，人工审核无法在批量规模上满足功能安全评估需求；基于阈值的工具只能报告偏差发生却无法识别其性质与来源；数据驱动分类器虽准确但依赖大规模标注数据且决策不透明，与ISO 26262要求的可追溯性冲突。因此需要一种兼具数据效率与可解释性的故障检测与诊断方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 在dSPACE实时平台上进行自动化需求检查，仅隔离违反安全需求的记录；(2) 采用滑动窗口将信号降维为统计、关系和上下文描述符，嵌入固定提示模板，通过4比特低秩适配（QLoRA风格的LoRA）微调指令式LLM，将其映射到故障位置并生成书面诊断理由。对四个模型家族（2B至8B参数）进行适配与测试，基于汽油机案例覆盖六种注入故障类别。

Result: 最小模型（2B参数）达到与最大模型（8B参数）相同的81.6%准确率；一个参数规模相近的模型未能收敛，表明在任务特定适配下诊断能力取决于收敛性而非参数数量。整个适配与评估周期可在单个商用加速器上运行。

Conclusion: 开源指令微调LLM可作为HIL验证中数据高效且可解释的故障检测与诊断引擎，其性能主要由训练收敛性决定而非模型规模，同时具备硬件需求低、可追溯性强的优势。

Abstract: The pre-series validation of automotive software on hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) platforms produces large volumes of multivariate sensor recordings whose assessment against functional safety requirements exceeds what manual review can sustain at campaign scale. Threshold-based tooling reports that a deviation has occurred but neither identifies its nature nor locates its source, while data-driven classifiers, although accurate, rely on large labelled datasets and return opaque decisions that sit uneasily with the traceability demanded by ISO 26262. This study examines whether open-source instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs), given a textual description of sensor behaviour, can serve as data-efficient and interpretable engines for fault detection and diagnosis inside the validation loop. A two-phase framework is proposed: automated requirement checking on a dSPACE real-time platform first isolates the recordings that violate a safety requirement, and only these are inspected, with sliding windows of the signals reduced to statistical, relational, and contextual descriptors, embedded in a fixed prompt, and mapped by a 4-bit low-rank-adapted LLM to a fault location accompanied by a written justification. Four model families ranging from two to eight billion parameters were adapted and tested on a gasoline-engine case study spanning six injected fault classes. The smallest model matched the largest at 81.6\% accuracy, whereas a comparably sized model failed to converge, indicating that diagnostic competence under task-specific adaptation follows convergence rather than parameter count, with the entire adapt-and-evaluate cycle fitting on a single commodity accelerator.

</details>


### [200] [AgentExecutor: Partial Code Execution via Agentic Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05959)
*Junkai Chen,Chengran Yang,Xing Hu,Zhenhao Li,Xin Xia,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentExecutor，一个基于多智能体（LLM agents）的偏代码执行框架，通过三阶段设计（执行环境准备、动态探索与迭代精化、前缀进化程序合成）来解决任意代码片段的执行难题，在代码覆盖率上超越现有最先进方法 Treefix 13.8%-19.9%，同时显著降低执行时间和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏代码执行方法（如 LExecutor 和 Treefix）虽然利用语言模型推断缺失信息，但存在两大局限：(i) 动作空间和反馈机制有限，难以执行诸如创建资源文件、解析环境配置等多样化操作；(ii) 采用僵化的优化策略，限制了执行的有效性和效率。因此需要一种能够自主探索更丰富动作空间并采用自适应优化策略的框架。

Method: AgentExecutor 采用三阶段设计：(1) 执行环境准备阶段；(2) 动态探索与迭代精化阶段，借助 LLM agents 的"思考-行动-反馈"循环自主探索更丰富的动作空间，支持创建资源文件、解析环境配置等操作；(3) 前缀进化阶段，通过程序合成改进代码执行质量。同时采用覆盖引导的上下文剪枝和前缀进化等自适应优化策略。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的数据集（Stack Overflow 代码片段和开源项目代码）上，AgentExecutor 达到最高 94% 和 90% 的代码覆盖率，较 Treefix 分别提升 19.9% 和 13.8%；执行时间最多降低 80.3%，成本最多降低 56.6%。

Conclusion: AgentExecutor 通过多智能体协作和自适应优化策略，为偏代码执行提供了有效且高效的解决方案，在代码覆盖率、执行时间和成本方面均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Executing code snippets is essential for dynamic program analysis, but it remains challenging to execute an arbitrary code snippet due to issues like missing context and incomplete dependencies. Existing approaches to partial code execution, such as LExecutor and Treefix, leverage the power of language models to infer missing information and enable execution. However, they suffer from (i) limited action spaces and feedback, and (ii) rigid optimization strategies, which restrict their effectiveness and efficiency. In this paper, we propose AgentExecutor, a novel multi-agent framework for partial code execution. Our approach introduces a three-phase design: execution environment preparation, dynamic exploration with iterative refinement, and prefix evolution via program synthesis. Supported by the power of LLM agents who can think, act, and get feedback iteratively, AgentExecutor is able to autonomously explore a richer action space, enabling diverse operations such as creating resource files and resolving environment configuration. Furthermore, it adopts adaptive optimization strategies, including coverage-guided context pruning and prefix evolution via program synthesis, to systematically improve the execution quality of partial code. We evaluate AgentExecutor on two widely used datasets comprising Stack Overflow snippets and open-source project code. The results show that AgentExecutor achieves up to 94% and 90% code coverage, outperforming the state-of-the-art approach Treefix by 19.9% and 13.8%, respectively. In addition, AgentExecutor significantly reduces execution time (by up to 80.3%) and cost (by up to 56.6%). These findings demonstrate that AgentExecutor provides an effective and efficient solution for partial code execution.

</details>


### [201] [LangChoiceBench: Measuring and Explaining Programming-Language Choice in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06041)
*Lukas Twist,Twm Stone,Helen Yannakoudakis,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍LangChoiceBench，一个用于衡量大语言模型在项目级代码生成中对Python语言偏好的基准测试，覆盖28个项目和7个软件领域。对25个LLM的评估发现Python被过度选择、推荐与实现一致性低、小型开放权重模型Python偏好更强且语言多样性更低。分析9,826条推理轨迹还发现了"幻影证据"和推理与代码语言不一致等失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM在生成项目级代码时表现出强烈的Python偏好，但目前缺乏系统化的基准来跨模型测量这一行为。构建该基准旨在填补这一空白，以量化和理解模型的语言选择偏差。

Method: 构建LangChoiceBench基准，覆盖28个项目和7个软件领域（这些领域Python通常不是最佳默认选择），评估25个多样的LLM，并分析9,826条推理轨迹以理解语言选择背后的机制和失败模式。

Result: 发现Python仍被严重过度选择；推荐与实现之间的一致性较低；较小的开放权重模型通常表现出更强的Python偏好和更低的语言多样性；大多数Python选择是自动的或由"易用性"驱动，而非基于项目需求的明确考量；部分案例存在制造上下文支持Python选择的"幻影证据"失败模式，以及推理中选择的语言与实际生成代码语言相矛盾的情况。

Conclusion: LLM在项目级代码生成中存在系统性的Python语言偏好偏差，这一偏差在小型开放权重模型中更为显著。需要关注模型的语言选择推理质量，特别是"幻影证据"和推理-实现不一致等失败模式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been shown to exhibit strong Python preferences when generating project-level code, but there is currently no systematic way to measure this behaviour across new models. To bridge this gap, we introduce LangChoiceBench, a project-level code-generation benchmark for measuring Python preference, recommendation-implementation consistency, and language diversity. LangChoiceBench covers 28 projects across seven software areas where Python is often a poor default. We evaluate 25 diverse LLMs and find that Python remains heavily over-selected, recommendation-implementation consistency is low, and smaller open-weight models generally show stronger Python preference and lower language diversity. We further analyse 9,826 reasoning traces and find that most Python choices are automatic or driven primarily by ease, rather than explicit consideration of project requirements. In a smaller but important set of cases, models fabricate contextual support for choosing Python - a failure mode we call phantom evidence - or produce code that contradicts the language selected in their own reasoning.

</details>


### [202] [In Terms of Explainability: Refining Requirements for Self-Explainable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06049)
*Arno Leue,Akhila Bairy,Maike Schwammberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对自主软件密集型系统中可解释性缺乏统一定义和开发指南的问题，提出了一种统一的可解释性定义体系，并结合现有定义分析和综合，提出了构建（自）可解释系统所需的结构化可解释性需求，并将解释正确性纳入统一定义，以支持正式标准的制定和系统认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主和软件密集型系统日益复杂且承担更多责任，但透明性和可解释性等属性缺乏统一的定义和开发指导。现有标准如欧盟AI法案和IEEE透明标准7001-2021已认识到对可解释性标准的需求，但目前仍不存在普遍适用的定义和开发指南，这阻碍了可解释系统的标准化、认证和审计。

Method: 首先对现有可解释性定义进行系统分析和组合，提出统一的可解释性定义；其次提出构建（自）可解释系统所需的结构化可解释性需求；通过分析由此产生的分类体系（taxonomy），将解释优良性（explanation goodness）及解释正确性纳入统一的定义体系之中。

Result: 形成了统一的可解释性定义体系，提出了结构化可解释性需求及分类框架，并将解释的正确性纳入统一定义，为（自）可解释系统的正式标准制定提供了基础。

Conclusion: 通过建立统一的可解释性分类体系，能够支持（自）可解释系统正式标准的开发，并为可解释系统的认证或审计迈出有益的一步。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [203] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于CLI的软件工程Agent在单一训练脚手架（OpenHands）轨迹数据下微调导致跨脚手架性能显著退化的问题，提出规划结构是导致该差距的关键因素，并引入DCAS（后端替换拦截层）实现跨脚手架评估与规划感知轨迹收集，证明将规划从固定脚手架工件转变为可学习的模型能力可有效弥合跨脚手架性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源生态中的软件工程Agent训练轨迹几乎全部在OpenHands脚手架下收集，导致微调模型在该脚手架下表现良好但在其他非训练脚手架上部署时性能大幅退化，而未训练的基座模型不存在这种差异，表明差距由微调引入且与训练脚手架的约定密切相关。作者假设负载关键的脚手架特定行为是规划结构（包括显式规划和隐式规划），解决之道是将规划从固定脚手架工件转变为模型的可学习能力。

Method: 作者提出DCAS（Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding），一个后端替换拦截层，可在不修改脚手架的前提下路由任何CLI脚手架与任何后端模型之间的API流量，实现跨脚手架评估和规划感知的轨迹收集。通过受控的规划源干预实验验证规划质量是高杠杆因素，并在单一脚手架下收集少量DCAS规划感知轨迹对模型进行微调。

Result: 受控的规划源干预实验证实规划质量是高杠杆组件，其带来的增益超过观察到的跨脚手架性能差距。使用DCAS收集的少量规划感知轨迹微调的模型，在非训练脚手架上表现出一致性增益，且显式规划与隐式规划两种规划维度在训练数据中可经验性分离。

Conclusion: 跨脚手架性能退化由微调引入且与训练脚手架的规划结构约定密切相关；通过DCAS收集规划感知轨迹并将规划内化为模型能力，可以有效提升模型在任意非训练脚手架下的泛化性能，显式规划与隐式规划是可分离且可分别优化的。

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GSE（全局技能演化框架），通过技能关系图和基于聚类的技能整合，联合优化LLM智能体的技能兼容性与泛化性，在软件工程任务中显著优于现有技能演化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的技能演化方法通常将技能演化视为局部更新序列，忽略了技能之间的关系，且容易产生过拟合的技能更新，导致跨任务泛化能力差。

Method: GSE包含三个核心组件：1）维护技能关系图（SRG），显式建模技能的相互关系并共同演化；2）基于聚类的技能整合，从局部更新中抽象出可复用的能力；3）基于重放的验证机制，防止过拟合和行为回退。

Result: 在测试生成和假阳性报告过滤两个任务上，GSE在两个SOTA编码智能体（OpenHands和mini-SWE-agent）上均取得最优精确率、召回率和F1分数。相比现有方法，精确率提升6.1%~34.1%，召回率提升31.8%~180.0%（测试生成）；精确率提升15.4%~96.4%，召回率提升13.1%~19.8%（假阳性过滤）。工业部署中F1分数提升61.4%。

Conclusion: GSE通过全局化视角联合优化技能兼容性与泛化性，在多个编码智能体和任务上都验证了有效性与泛化能力，展示了从局部更新到全局技能演化的优势。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [205] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究采用实证主义方法，通过PLS-SEM分析识别了敏捷软件开发项目中的关键成功因素（CSF）与项目成功标准之间的关系，并开发了一个可用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管敏捷项目比传统软件开发项目管理方法更成功，但整体成功率仍然较低，大量项目仍被视为受挑战或失败。这种低成功率归因于项目未严格依据敏捷项目的关键成功因素（CSF）和当代项目成功标准进行评估。

Method: 采用实证主义（positivist）研究范式，通过敏捷从业者的视角调查敏捷软件开发项目的CSF。使用PLS-SEM（偏最小二乘结构方程模型）配合SmartPLS软件进行数据分析，并检验构念与项目成功标准之间关系的假设。

Result: 研究发现少量关键因素对敏捷项目成功具有显著贡献，并开发了一个可用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了可操作的新模型，用于评估和衡量敏捷项目成功，弥补了敏捷项目缺乏严格CSF评价体系的不足。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [206] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种当代通用大语言模型（LLMs）在零微调条件下，通过少样本提示策略将非结构化自然语言需求直接转换为线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式的能力，并在15个异构需求基准上进行了450个候选公式的系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键和任务关键系统的验证依赖于数学上精确的形式化规格，但将非结构化自然语言需求自动翻译为形式化规格（如LTL公式）仍然是需求工程和形式化方法领域的长期挑战，现有方法缺乏自动化且需要大量人工参与。

Method: 采用少样本提示（few-shot prompting）策略，对六个现代LLM在15个结构各异的自然语言需求基准上进行测试；每个需求-模型对收集5次独立生成，共产生450个候选LTL公式；性能通过人工语义评估、pass@k（k=1,3,5）以及衡量随机试验间句法可复现性的自一致性指标进行评估。

Result: 结果表明，当前通用LLM无需任务特定微调即可在非结构化自然语言到LTL的转换任务上达到实际有意义的性能水平；此外，通过将生成的公式与模型生成的自然语言解释配对，以及讨论基于时间线的LTL可视化补充使用，提升了对非专家用户的可理解性。

Conclusion: 现代LLM正逐渐成为半自动化形式化工作流中可行的前端助手，为弥合自然语言需求与形式化规格之间的鸿沟提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.
